  conditions
    always
      ΓêÜ should always return true
    never
      ΓêÜ should always return false
    allOf
      ΓêÜ should return true if all of the arguments is true
      ΓêÜ should return false if one of the arguments is false
    oneOf
      ΓêÜ should return true if one of the arguments is true
      ΓêÜ should return true if more than one of the arguments is true
      ΓêÜ should return false if none of the arguments are true
    not
      ΓêÜ should return true if the argument is false
      ΓêÜ should return false if the argument is true
    pathExact
      ΓêÜ should return true if request url is the same
      ΓêÜ should return false if request url is not the same
    pathMatch
      ΓêÜ should return true if request url matches
      ΓêÜ should return false if request url does not match
    expression
      ΓêÜ should return false if expression does not match
      ΓêÜ should pass if expression match
    method
      ΓêÜ should return true if methods param is string and matches
      ΓêÜ should return true if methods param is list and method is member
      ΓêÜ should return false if methods param is string and does not match
      ΓêÜ should return false if param is list and method is not member
    tlsClientAuthenticated
      ΓêÜ should return true if request is client authenticated
      ΓêÜ should return false if request is client authenticated
    jsonSchema
      with a local schema
        ΓêÜ should return true if the body matches the schema
        ΓêÜ should return false if the body does not match the schema
      with a remote schema
        33) should match if the body matches the schema
        34) should not match if the body does not matches the schema
    complex conditions
      ΓêÜ correctly handles complex conditional rule
